| 2026-09-11 | Build S0.4: the editor window, the scene, one callout | `host/src/editor.rs` owns the window and the region the page may see: a region-at-a-scale service that is a 1:1 copy at actual size and a downscale of only what is on screen at fit, which is part 5's display policy; a synthetic checkerboard probe whose whole purpose is to make resampling visible; and a command that captures the window's own area of the screen so the first-frame question can be answered by the screen instead of the page. `editor/index.html` is the scene: the canvas, plus a callout layer laid out in IMAGE pixels with one CSS transform for zoom, so wrapping cannot change with zoom, and three explicit direction modes resolved per bubble. `editor/editor-checks.js` is the evidence. | Checks: fmt clean, clippy with -D warnings clean, build green, 14 unit tests pass, and the 26 editor checks all pass in the shipping configuration with no browser flags. Two host defects were real and are fixed: the region's dimensions travelled as response headers, which a cross-origin fetch cannot read without being told to expose them, so the page refused a perfectly good buffer; and the page believed devicePixelRatio, which this web view reports as 1 on a 225% display, so the ratio is now measured against the host's own numbers. Two findings are open and recorded: F43, the fit view costs 1.1 s of resampling, and F44, the window is not DPI-scaled so the interface renders at a third of the size the display asks for.
